*   **Core Feature**: A data processing pipeline that ingests PDF/Excel bank statements, uses an external OCR API (Upstage Solar) for PDFs, parses and standardizes the data, and saves it to a PostgreSQL database for analysis and financial tracking.

**key DB schema**
*   **Transaction**: Stores individual transaction records. Columns likely include uid=0(root) gid=0(root) groups=0(root), Thu Mar 19 05:28:27 UTC 2026, , , , , , , , .
*   **TransactionTemplate**: Stores parsing configurations for different bank statement formats.
*   **Loan**, **TrackedItem**: Models supporting the loan tracking feature.
